{"forms":[{"form":"categories","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"This steep and dangerous climb belongs to the most difficult category.","type":"example"},{"text":"I wouldn’t put this book in the same category as the author’s first novel.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A group, often named or numbered, to which items are assigned based on similarity or defined criteria."],"synonyms":[{"word":"class"},{"word":"family"},{"word":"genus"},{"word":"kingdom"},{"word":"order"},{"word":"pigeonhole"},{"word":"phylum"},{"word":"race"},{"word":"tribe"},{"word":"type"},{"word":"category"},{"word":"class"},{"word":"description"},{"word":"stripe"},{"tags":["informal"],"word":"department"},{"word":"fashion"},{"word":"division"},{"word":"family"},{"word":"genre"},{"word":"ilk"},{"word":"kind"},{"word":"likes"},{"word":"race"},{"word":"rubric"},{"word":"set"},{"word":"sort"},{"word":"type"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"One well-known category has sets as objects and functions as arrows.","type":"example"},{"text":"Just as a monoid consists of an underlying set with a binary operation \"on top of it\" which is closed, associative and with an identity, a category consists of an underlying digraph with an arrow composition operation \"on top of it\" which is transitively closed, associative, and with an identity at each object. In fact, a category's composition operation, when restricted to a single one of its objects, turns that object's set of arrows (which would all be loops) into a monoid.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A collection of objects, together with a transitively closed collection of composable arrows between them, such that every object has an identity arrow, and such that arrow composition is associative."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.ɪ.ɡ(ə.)ɹi/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.ɪˌɡoɹ.i/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰæɾ.ɪˌɡoɹ.i]","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.əˌɡoɹ.i/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰæɾ.əˌɡoɹ.i]","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.ɪˌɡoɹ.i/","tags":["California"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰäɾ.ɪˌɡoɹ.i]","tags":["California"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.əˌɡoɹ.i/","tags":["California"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰäɾ.əˌɡoɹ.i]","tags":["California"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkæt.ɪ.ɡ(ə.)ɹi/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰæɾ.ɪ.ɡ(ə.)ɹi]","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkɛt.ɘ.ɡ(ɘ.)ɹi/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkɛt.ɘˌɡoː.ɹi/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/kaˈtɛˌɡoɹi/","tags":["Philippines"]}],"upstream_line":222336,"upstream_offset":1078792687,"upstream_record_sha256":"18d95f7f76be5bdd00534450430ed6c0bedc57b364ff7ce47ababa279437240c","word":"category"}
